# The line bank: one-on-one openings, more versions (dialogue D2; format in
# src/world/folk/voice.rs). Same keys as players.txt. The opening is two
# parts said as one line: his mood (talk.open.sour/good/flat), then the
# subject (talk.open.<subject>). Mood parts stay short and never name a
# subject; subject parts never assume a mood. Facts: name (his first name),
# full, coach (Coach + your last name), you, pos, team, friend, subject.
